# Skills · A · part 313

863 entries, auto to auto-empirical-research-skills. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **auto**: Automate one Jira ticket end to end with no stops — understand it across the services, create Draft Allure cases, write and run the automated tests (the acceptance criteria are the oracle; a failing test is a bug), review, commit and push a ticket branch, and hand back a pre-filled draft-PR link. Use when the user types /auto &lt;KEY&gt; and walks away. ([cristian-robert/copilot-harness](https://github.com/cristian-robert/copilot-harness/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/auto))
- **auto**: Autonomous (unattended, e.g. overnight) orchestrator run (/orchestrator:auto). Start contract with the user once, then an external supervisor works alone in a dedicated git worktree on branch auto/&lt;id&gt; (checkpoint commits, never a push, narrow generated permission allowlist), waits through usage limits instead of failing, and writes only files until the morning report. Subcommands - start (default), status, report, review, pause, continue, stop, takeover, abort. ([czekanskyy/claude-orchestrator](https://github.com/czekanskyy/claude-orchestrator/tree/HEAD/plugins/orchestrator/skills/auto))
- **auto**: Work the repository's issues one after another, from open to merged — write the next issue's acceptance criteria when it lacks them, then dispatch build, the pull request review and merge-pr's gate per issue in fresh subagents and merge in this session, stop on the first issue that does not merge, and finish by running cleanup. Use when asked to work through the backlog, clear the board, or keep going until a count or a deadline. ([dean-matt/claude-skills](https://github.com/dean-matt/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/github-sdlc/skills/auto))
